## [3.34.2] - 2026-10-08 (October 2026)

### Added
- **Neighborhood Decor in the house editor companion.** Under Collections, beside Recently
  Learned, Dyeable Items and Trophies, a new list holds the decor sold by vendors in Founder's
  Point and Razorwind Shores: the same pieces the Neighborhood filter finds on the Acquire tab.
  It's among the Styles tab's collections too.
- **When something inside HDG breaks, it now says so in chat.** A line starting `[HDG] [E]`
  names what went wrong, so you can screenshot it and send it to us on Discord. Some of you have
  had the window stop opening from the minimap button, or the Zone Scanner refuse to close, with
  no error anywhere: the game hides addon errors unless you turn them on, so there was nothing to
  report. This release doesn't fix that yet. It lets us find the cause the next time it happens.
  Each problem prints once per session. (Thanks txcreek, Vectality and Panseit.)

### Fixed
- **The red plus in the house editor's catalog marks only decor you don't own.** Go straight
  into the editor without opening HDG and it could sit on nearly every piece, a mural with three
  in storage included, until you opened HDG. It now goes by the same count the game shows on
  that tile.

## [3.34.1] - 2026-09-22 (September 2026)

### Fixed
- **The shopping list keeps its vendor headings when you send a blueprint's missing decor over
  twice.** The second send rebuilt the list while the window was open, and the items landed
  before anyone had worked out who sells them, so they all fell into the Wishlist and the
  vendor headings disappeared until you closed the window and opened it again. Sellers are
  now worked out the moment a list arrives, however it arrives -- routed from a blueprint,
  pasted in as a code, or sent by another addon. (Thanks Soul.)
- **The Destroy decor list holds still while you destroy from it.** It sorts by how many copies
  you have stored, so every destroy reshuffled it and could slide a different piece under your
  cursor between clicks. From your first destroy the order stays put; it sorts afresh when you
  leave the tab and come back, or turn the filter off and on.

## [3.34.0] - 2026-09-19 (September 2026)

### Added
- **Ctrl-Shift-click a decor row to destroy one stored copy.** With the Destroy decor filter
  on, each Ctrl-Shift-click in the Decor browser destroys one copy of that row, plain or dyed,
  with no dialog to confirm. Clicking faster than the game confirms simply queues more copies
  of the same row. The row's tooltip lists it with the other clicks. Suggested by Soul.
- **The destroy dialog steps by ten and by a hundred, and reaches every stored copy.** -100,
  -10, +10 and +100 sit either side of the count, and the old limit of 99 at a time is gone,
  so a big stack of stored copies can be cleared in a few clicks. Copies are destroyed at a
  pace the game keeps up with, and the count only moves when the game confirms each one: the
  dialog stays open with that live count, and Cancel becomes Stop. The game pauses after about
  26 destroys in a row; the dialog says so and keeps trying every few seconds until the game
  takes them again. If it never does, the run ends and says how many were really destroyed.

### Fixed
- **The bulk-buy picker's text stays inside its window.** A run that stopped early wrote its
  reason on the same line as the count, running past both edges. The reason now sits on its own
  line, and every line under the wheels wraps to fit.
- **A blueprint's dyes now reach its shopping list.** Route to Shopping sent them, but the list
  turned away anything that was not decor, so a build's dyes never showed up. They now land in
  the Auction House section, where dyes are bought (or craft them from herbs at the dye station).
  The Styles card for that list still shows only the decor.
- **HDG's decor data refreshes on every tab that shows what you own.** With the window open
  across a /reload, the Blueprints, Styles, Recipes, Warehouse, Mogul and Add Decor tabs could
  keep HDG's first read of your collection, taken before the game had finished loading your
  storage, until you switched to another tab.
- **Shopping list items come off as you buy them, however you buy them.** Only Buy All used to
  take items off the list: right-clicking at the vendor, splitting a stack or confirming an
  expensive item left the list asking for pieces you already had. Now every purchase comes off
  the list you have showing once it arrives. An item on several lists only comes off the one
  you are shopping from, and a purchase that fails leaves the list alone. Reported by Soul.

### Changed
- **Blueprint dye counts now cover the whole build.** The game's count for a dye only covers
  decor you already have, so it kept climbing as you bought the build's pieces: 35 orange
  needed became 75 after buying 40 pillars. Each dye row now shows how many you hold out of
  what the build uses, counting your bags and banks, and "need" is the rest. The missing
  totals and the cost to build use the same numbers. Hovering a dye shows
  how many are in your bags and banks, what the game's import window will say for the same
  dye, and why the two differ. When the import window is short only because the dyes are
  sitting in your bank, it tells you how many to move into your bags. Suggested by Windgrace90.

## [3.33.2] - 2026-09-15 (September 2026)

### Fixed
- **Blueprint counts no longer go stale after a shopping trip.** A blueprint's have/need
  counts are the game's answer at the moment you inspected it. Working through several codes
  and buying as you went, the ones inspected earlier kept reporting pieces you had since
  bought, and buying off those counts meant duplicates. Now any change to your decor storage
  marks every inspected blueprint out of date: selecting one re-fetches it, and a Refresh
  button beside the share code lights up with "manifest out of date" while the one on screen
  needs it. Reported by Soul.
- **The paste box empties after a code is accepted.** Pasting a second code no longer lands
  on the end of the first. A code that is rejected stays in the box so it can be corrected.
  Reported by Soul.
- **A catalog that failed its first load now recovers on its own.** In 3.33.1 a storage
  change arriving while the first catalog build was still settling was thrown away instead of
  kept, so a failed or empty first load could sit at "loading" for the session when only the
  Merchant overlay or the editor companion was open. The rebuild is now remembered and run.
- **Nudging decor you already own no longer records it as newly learned.** Storage events
  parked during a failed first load were replayed against an index that had never been built,
  so every owned piece you moved in the editor wrote a false "learned" entry into Recent
  activity and the velocity estimates.
- **Labels show current values when a view is revealed.** Since the window started building
  on first open, a label could keep its build-time text on a view switch, on a revisit of a
  view whose data changed while hidden, or on a floating window's first open.
- **Opening HDG for the first time in combat waits for combat to end.** The window build
  makes a call the game blocks for addons in combat, which surfaced as "Interface action
  failed because of an AddOn". The build now runs when combat ends and the window shows then.
- **An error during login can no longer stall the game's memory collector.** The boot memory
  marks only hold the collector while the profiler is on, and release it even if a boot phase
  errors.

### Changed
- **Link in chat moved to the bottom of the blueprint list**, opposite Save a Blueprint, and
  it dims until a blueprint is selected. It was crowding the share-code row.

## [3.33.1] - 2026-09-14 (September 2026)

### Changed
- **Blueprint dye rows explain their counts.** Hovering a dye in a blueprint notes that the
  count comes from the game and only includes dyes in your bags, so dyes in your Warband Bank
  aren't counted. Move them to your bags before importing. Reported by Madailein Hatter.
- **Memory tuning across the addon.** HDG allocates about 40% less over a session and its
  peak is a third lower. The window itself is now built the first time you open it, so a
  session where you never open HDG carries 13 megabytes less from login. Opening the window no longer paints every tab, only the one on screen;
  the layout engine skips work that cannot change what you see; the Decor and Acquire lists
  build much lighter rows; a tab you leave releases its cached lists after a few minutes; the
  login catalog build runs once instead of twice and copies less; and a dozen smaller hot spots
  (the pet index, the Goblin table, the Warehouse materials list, Map All, the placed-decor
  search counter) do their work once instead of on every tick. Nothing on screen changes.

### Fixed
- **The House tab no longer leaks memory while it is open.** Every redraw of the dashboard
  built a fresh set of frames for its cards and abandoned the old ones, which the game cannot
  free. Each card now keeps its frames and repaints them in place. The dashboard is also only
  rebuilt while the House tab or a Projects view is on screen, and a placement nudge that
  changes no count no longer rebuilds the Decor list.
- **The vendor header's Wowhead button is back.** In the Acquire tab's vendor view it had been
  hidden for good. It shows again whenever a vendor is selected.
- **The Decor detail card no longer clips its bottom rows.** A crafted piece that is also
  tagged, stored and destroyable shows every meta row at once; the 3D preview gives up 28
  pixels so all seven fit.
- **A decor learned just after a failed catalog load no longer stays marked as needed.** HDG
  kept waiting for a load that had already given up and held every later learn aside until a
  reload. Those learns are now applied straight away.

## [3.33.0] - 2026-09-11 (September 2026)

### Added
- **A Blueprint Library.** The Library button on the Blueprints tab opens every code you have
  pasted and every blueprint in your catalog as one list you can search and sort by name,
  source, type or date. A tick box keeps the game's automatic saves out of it. Each row shows
  when the blueprint was pasted or saved and the note you left on it, and the notes field
  takes whatever you want to remember about a build. The picker beside it is tidier too:
  "Pasted codes" and "Your catalog" now fold away and carry their counts, Save a Blueprint
  sits under the list instead of crowding the header, and the 50-blueprint limit is read from
  the game rather than assumed. Reported by Soul.
- **Shift-click an item in the Shopping List to search the Auction House for it.** With the
  Auction House open on a Buy tab, the item's name lands in the search bar, the same gesture
  the Warehouse and Recipes materials lists already have. Right-click still opens the item menu.
- **The neighbourhood pet decor vendors' eight pieces now show where to buy them.** The
  Cherished Pet's Rug, Cozy Bird Nest, Silvermoon Dragonhawk Incubator, the Westfall, Crossroads
  and Crude Pet Cages, the Cozy Lightbloom Lilypad and the Loyal Companion's Plinth had no
  source at all. Perry Winkles in Founder's Point and Agratha in Razorwind Shores each sell all
  eight for 50 gold, and Agratha now has a map pin. Reported by KevinW.
- **The six pieces inside the Last Architect's weekly gift now name the quest.** The Ogre
  Champion's Prized Skull, Sin'dorei Golden Welcome, Masterful Sin'dorei Gravestone, Elegant
  Enchanted Vanity, Mastercrafted Fungal Row Planter and Revered Deepstone Table each come
  from "A Curated Gift", the weekly quest at The Last Architect, whose Arcanostorage Sphere
  holds one of the six at random. Reported by KevinW.
- **The Cosmic Void Gravitational Orb now names both caches it can drop from,** the Victorious
  Stormarion Cache as well as the Pinnacle Cache.

### Changed
- **The sidebar shows which groups fold.** Every group in the left nav has been collapsible
  all along, but the toggle was an unmarked click on the group icon. A chevron now sits at the
  right edge of each group row, House folds like the others, and the Tools row folds when
  clicked anywhere. Reported by Soul.

### Fixed
- **Clicking a vendor now scrolls the vendor list to them.** From the zone scanner, the
  shopping list or a decor item's source, the jump highlighted the vendor but left the list
  wherever it was, so a vendor near the bottom meant scrolling and remembering the name. The
  row now scrolls into view, the vendor's goods appear straight away instead of after a second
  click, and the jump also clears any Shop by Vendor filters that would have hidden that vendor,
  which used to land you on a different vendor's goods instead. Reported by ReganB.
- **A blueprint's missing count now says how many pieces, not how many kinds.** "17 items to
  acquire first" meant 17 kinds of decor, some needed six times over. The verdict now counts
  pieces, the list header shows both, and each group header carries its piece count. Reported
  by Soul.
- **Forgetting a pasted blueprint code now asks first,** the same as deleting a saved
  blueprint. The code keeps working and can be pasted again, but the name you gave it goes
  with it, and the list was the only place the code was kept. Reported by Soul.
- **Import as Set no longer drops the decor you already own when Missing only is on.** The
  furnishing set was built from the filtered view, so it held only the missing pieces.
- **Deleting a second character from the Alts list in one session no longer names and deletes
  the first one.** The confirmation dialog kept its first character.
- **Deleting a layout or a what-if design now asks first.** Both the Layouts view's Delete
  button and the Architect's version menu removed the design outright; a design has no way
  back in, so they confirm like every other delete. Clearing a shopping list asks too, the
  same as deleting one.
- **The blueprint house picker no longer shows a neighbourhood as a house name.** An unnamed
  house now reads "House in Founder's Point" instead of borrowing the neighbourhood's name.
- **Delete confirmations on the Styles tab name the right kind of collection.** The first kind
  deleted in a session set the wording for every kind after it.
- **Rename boxes open on the current name.** Renaming a design in Projects, a layout in the
  Layouts view or a style opened an empty box; each now starts from the name it has.
- **Arrow keys through a list no longer re-centre it on every keypress** when the next row is
  already on screen.
- **A source only HDG knows about is now named, not just chipped.** The Elodor Barrel and
  the Draenei Farmer's Trellis showed a bare [QUST] in the Decor panel with no quest behind
  it, and every quest, drop or treasure the game's catalog leaves blank did the same. The
  source line, the catalog tooltip and a copied blueprint list now carry the name, as they
  already did for vendors. Reported by KevinW.
- **The Apply to House tooltip no longer claims the Automatic Save uses a blueprint slot.** The
  game's automatic backup of your previous layout sits outside the 50 slots, and HDG's slot
  counter already left it out.

## [3.32.3] - 2026-09-07 (September 2026)

### Added
- **The Brewfest Stein and Brewfest Crate now show where to buy them.** Both are sold for
  100 gold at the neighbourhood holiday hut, by Holly Twinklebright in Founder's Point and
  Jolli Maxiboom in Razorwind Shores, and both vendors now have map pins.

### Fixed
- **Decor paid for with an item no longer shows a gold price.** The Square Stormpike Table
  costs 1 Mark of Honor and the Moonclasp Satchel 500 Dreamsurge Coalescence, but HDG read
  both as gold, so they also slipped into the "Gold, No Unlocks" filter. The 25 pieces the
  catalog prices in items now show that item's icon in the cost line, the vendor list, the
  blueprint cost badge and the catalog tooltip, and the gold-only filter leaves them out.
- **The Well-Lit Incontinental Loveseat no longer looks freely buyable.** Ando the Gat sells
  it for 1000 gold, but only at Renown rank 12 with the Gallagio Loyalty Rewards Club,
  which the game's decor catalog does not mention anywhere. It now carries that
  reputation gate and drops out of the "Gold, No Unlocks" filter.
- **The Raw materials view for a crafting queue no longer asks for six-figure mote counts.**
  The four Midnight motes can each be transmuted from ten of another, in a ring, and HDG
  treated any gathered reagent with such a recipe as something to craft, multiplying by ten
  at every step. Gathered, vendor-sold and dropped reagents are now where the raw view
  stops, and a recipe that makes several per craft, like Imbued Silkweave at ten, is
  counted as one craft rather than ten.
- **Six vendor-sold reagents no longer sit under "From Gathering".** Distilled Algari
  Freshwater, Inconceivably Aged Vinegar, Laestrite Setting, Perfect Preservatives, Smuggled
  Azerothian Produce and Telmor-Aruuna Hard Cheese now list under "From Vendor". Stabilized
  Derivate, an Alchemy recycling by-product, moves to "From Crafted".

## [3.32.2] - 2026-09-06 (September 2026)

### Fixed
- **The house editor companion keeps your place in a collection after you place a piece.**
  Placing from a collection longer than a page sent the list back to the top every time;
  it now stays where you were, and the same holds when you pick a piece back up.

## [3.32.1] - 2026-09-06 (September 2026)

### Changed
- **HDG no longer catalogues your neighbours' decor as you walk past their plots.** The
  live Placed Decor list only ever shows the house or plot you are standing in, but the
  part of HDG feeding it was collecting every plot that came into view across a whole
  neighbourhood. It now only listens while you are inside your own house or on your own plot.

### Fixed
- **A decor learned right after buying it no longer stays marked as needed.** The vendor
  marks and the Decor list took a just-learned item's identity from a catalog record
  that can be blank for a few moments after you acquire it, so the learn was dropped until
  a reload. They now take it from the game's own storage update. A learn that lands while
  the catalog is still doing its first scan of the session is also applied once the scan
  finishes instead of being overwritten by it.
- **The blueprint cost badge no longer runs off the panel.** A build priced in many
  currencies stretched the "cost to build" line past the right edge of the detail card.
  It now shows the first three and "+N more"; hover it to see every currency, each
  named beside its amount, since several housing currencies share a near-identical icon.

## [3.32.0] - 2026-09-05 (September 2026)

### Added
- **My Styles can be grouped under categories you create.** In Styles -> Browse, hit
  "+ New Category" on the My Styles bar, then fill it from its "+ Add styles" button or
  file a single style from the new plus button on its row. Categories remember whether
  you left them open, and styles that aren't in one sit in their own fold at the top.
- **The house editor companion groups your styles the same way.** Its Your Styles list
  shows your categories, a fold for styles not in one, and your Filtered Sets under their
  own heading; each folds with a click and remembers it, independently of the Browse tab.

### Changed
- **Styles section bars show a chevron and their count.** The bars could always be
  clicked to fold a section; now they say so, and the count that was painted off-screen
  since the tab shipped is back on it.

### Fixed
- **The Browse search box now filters the list.** It was writing to a slot the list never
  read. It also looks inside collapsed categories and says how many matched.
- **Opening or closing a Styles section no longer jumps the list back to the top**, and
  which sections you left open is remembered between sessions instead of resetting to
  all-closed on every login.

## [3.31.2] - 2026-09-03 (September 2026)

### Fixed
- **The three-second freeze at login is gone, and so is the one at first open.**
  Two things met. Since 3.31.1, the first read of the decor catalogue also
  asked the game to match every achievement name it knows -- some four thousand
  lookups, three seconds with the game held still -- so that achievement links
  would work for decor the addon had no ID for. On 12.1 that turned out to be
  eight pieces. The addon now ships those IDs, resolved from the game's own
  data, and never asks the client to match names again: the same read that
  took 3.2 seconds takes about a tenth of one. And since July the zone alert
  had been triggering that read the moment a character logged in, so the
  freeze landed on every character, every login. The catalogue is now left
  alone at login, as everything else in the addon is, and is read when you
  first open the window, visit a vendor or hover a decor tooltip; the alert for
  the zone you are standing in fires at that point instead. Reported by
  themindboggle.

- **Torv Dubstomp's decor is no longer credited to Ransa Greyfeather.** Twelve
  Highmountain pieces -- the Large Highmountain Drum, the Skyhorn kites, the
  Skyhorn Storage Chest, the Tauren Vertical Windmill and eight more -- named
  Ransa Greyfeather, the Highmountain Tribe emissary in Thunder Totem, as their
  seller, and her Shop by Vendor page listed every one of them. She does not
  stock them: she sells only the eight reputation pieces, and the twelve come
  from Torv Dubstomp, the Decor Specialist a few steps from her. The decor card,
  the source line and Shop by Vendor now all say Torv, and her page lists only
  what she actually sells. Reported by reganart.

## [3.31.1] - 2026-09-02 (September 2026)

### Fixed
- **Decor sold in both neighbourhoods names both of them, instead of one twice.**
  Timicky, Taifa and the Cursed Keepsake each stand in Founder's Point as well
  as Razorwind Shores, but the addon only knew one of the two -- so the other's
  listing landed on the wrong record and had its neighbourhood overwritten, and
  the item showed the same vendor and place on both lines. All sixteen Kobold
  Endeavor pieces did this. Each now has an entry in both neighbourhoods, with
  a map pin for each, where before three of those six entries had no
  coordinates at all. Reported by reganart.

- **Opening the window at a house you have not captured yet no longer throws an
  error.** The Architect's Section view worked out how wide to draw the house
  from the rooms it knows about, but decided whether there was anything to draw
  from the number of floors the game reports -- so standing at a house with
  nothing captured gave it a floor to draw and no width to draw it at. Reported
  by gnucleargnome, who hit it opening the window from a zone alert.

- **Dennia Silvertongue's shelves are no longer nearly empty.** Shop by Vendor
  listed seven of the thirty-four pieces she sells: the game files the rest
  under a catch-all "World Vendors", or under where the item first came from --
  the in-game shop, a Diablo IV promotion -- and never mention her. Her Paw Pal
  range, the whole Sanctuary Chess set, the bottles, the ice chest and the Perch
  of the Dawnfire Phoenix are now listed under her, with a map pin and a
  waypoint, while still showing where they originally came from. Reported by
  Vamoose.

- **The quantity picker now works on every item at a vendor, not just the first
  ten.** It was only attached to the first ten slots of a merchant page, so on a
  UI that shows a wider vendor window everything past the tenth item quietly
  ignored the right-click and bought the ordinary way instead. Reported by
  Vamoose, who could open it on the first painting at Dennia Silvertongue but
  not on the Dark Portal three rows down.

- **Buying decor that goes to your bags no longer stalls.** Some vendors sell
  decor for gold that arrives in your bags rather than in decor storage. The
  picker only watched decor storage for a gold purchase, so it waited for
  something that was never coming: the gold was spent, the item was in your
  bags, and it reported buying nothing. It now watches both, whatever you paid
  with. Reported by Alora at Dennia Silvertongue.

- **Clicking a decor's achievement opens the achievement again.** The game's
  catalog names an achievement but never gives its ID, and the addon only knew
  the IDs for a couple of hundred of them -- so for everything else the [ACH]
  line offered to open the achievement window and then did nothing at all. It
  now asks the game itself to match the name, which covers every achievement
  and works in any language. Where a name belongs to more than one achievement
  it stays unlinked rather than opening the wrong one, and an achievement it
  still cannot place reads as plain text instead of promising a click.

### Changed
- **Looking up where a recipe comes from no longer repaints the window.** It
  announced itself the first time it saw each recipe, which told everything on
  screen to redraw -- from inside the redraw that had just asked the question.
  The lookup answers instantly and had nothing to announce. Worth a few
  milliseconds a time rather than anything you would have noticed, but it was
  work the addon was doing for no reason.

## [3.31.0] - 2026-08-31 (August 2026)

### Added
- **The Architect can show your whole house at once.** A new Section view draws
  every floor stacked in place, seen from an angle, with dashed lines running up
  through the stairwells that join them. Floor plans show one storey at a time,
  so the things that live BETWEEN floors -- whether your stairs line up, what is
  sitting above what -- were the one thing you could never see. Click any floor's
  label to drop back into its plan and keep building. The button sits next to
  Capture all floors.

- **Crafted decor says where its recipe comes from.** The decor detail card
  gains a Recipe line under the profession -- "Profession Trainer -- Classic
  Alchemy (25)", or the vendor and zone that sells the pattern -- and the
  Recipes tab tooltip lists every source a recipe has, with the cost shown in
  its own coins and currency icons. The answer is the profession book's own,
  read from the game as you look rather than stored, so it stays right as the
  game changes. 305 of the 325 craftable pieces have one.

- **A copied requirements list says which neighbourhood its vendors are for,
  and you choose which as you copy it.** The copy window carries an
  Alliance/Horde switch, and the text gains a "Vendors listed for:" line naming
  the side it was written for. Some decor is sold by differently-named traders
  in Founder's Point and Razorwind Shores, so a shared list that does not say
  which one it means sends half its readers to the wrong place. Flipping the
  switch rewrites the list in front of you before you copy it. The choice
  belongs to that export and does not change where your own shopping list
  sends you.

### Fixed
- **Decor from the in-game shop or a promotion says so when you copy a
  blueprint's list.** 3.30.0 taught that list about delves, treasures and
  events and stopped one step short of these two, so a Naturally Elegant
  Doormat still copied out with nothing after its name.

## [3.30.0] - 2026-08-29 (August 2026)

### Added
- **The shopping list can be told which neighbourhood you shop in.** A small
  Alliance/Horde switch sits beside the list name, and where the same decor is
  sold in both Founder's Point and Razorwind Shores, it sends you to yours.
  Eighteen merchants keep a stall in both neighbourhoods, so this is usually the
  same trader either way -- it just stops the list routing you across the world
  for a rug you could buy at home. Either faction can shop in either place, so
  nothing is ever hidden: a trader who works nowhere else still appears, and
  flipping the switch re-sorts the list you already have rather than only the
  next one you import. Until you touch it, it follows your character. Reported
  by moondazze, who was sent to the Horde vendors for an Alliance house.

- **Shopping lists can be renamed.** A Rename button beside + New, opened with
  the current name selected so it is type-over-and-Enter. A list routed from a
  blueprint arrives named after the blueprint, which is right up until you have
  three of them. Asked for by moondazze.
- **The item and vendor counts moved to a rail under the list**, where the Decor
  and Pets tabs already keep theirs, instead of competing for room with the
  controls along the top.

### Fixed
- **A named trader is no longer passed over for "World Vendors".** Where a piece
  is sold both by someone standing in a zone and by the game's catch-all
  "World Vendors" listing, the catch-all sometimes came first -- so a copied
  blueprint list said to buy Flat Boulders from nobody in particular while
  Trevor Grenner was standing in Founder's Point selling them. The trader you
  can actually walk to now wins, and the catch-all is still listed when it is
  genuinely the only source. Reported by Madailein Hatter.
- **Decor that comes from a delve, a treasure, or an event says so when you copy
  a blueprint's list.** The Hanging Dawnflower knew perfectly well it was a
  Midnight Delves drop -- Find Decor had been saying so all along -- but the
  copied list left the line bare, because only vendors, quests, achievements and
  professions were ever written into it. All three now come through. Reported by
  Madailein Hatter.

## [3.29.1] - 2026-08-27 (August 2026)

### Fixed
- **The catalog tooltip names the colours of the tile you are hovering**, not
  every colour you own the item in. Each dyed variant is its own tile in the
  catalog; 3.29.0 listed all of them on every hover, which for a player with a
  hundred of one dyed table was the whole inventory each time.
## [3.29.0] - 2026-08-27 (August 2026)

### Added
- **The catalog tooltip lists your dyed copies.** Hover a piece in the house
  editor's catalog and, under the vendor lines, each colour you own it in is
  named in that dye's own colour with how many you have -- the way the
  Companion labels its dyed tiles. The catalog's own count lumps every colour
  together; this is the line that says which.
- **Pick a room, get the pets that suit it.** House > Pets has a fourth filter
  beside Clade, Family and Size: Room. Choose the Kitchen and you get rats,
  roaches, chickens and a cat -- about a hundred pets, not eight hundred -- with
  the ones that truly belong at the top and each row saying which it is:
  "belongs here", "fits", or "could work". Twenty-two rooms, from the Great Hall
  to the Stable, each one a hand-picked list rather than a rule -- so the Dungeon
  really is abominations and ghouls, the Aviary really is parrots, the Library
  really is owls, and yes, there is a Coffee Shop. Search and the other filters
  still work inside it.
- **And a Mood filter, in the same language the Styles tab speaks.** Rustic,
  Cozy, Nature, Arcane, Scholarly, Gothic, Void, Royal, Industrial, Seafaring,
  Whimsical, Primitive -- the moods you already sort decor by. Build a gothic
  room in Styles, come to Pets, ask for gothic, and get the bats and the
  gargoyles rather than every undead in the game.
- **Spiders are their own group.** They used to sit with the insects, which filed a
  tarantula and a butterfly in the same drawer. Spiders now have a group of their
  own, and the card says what one actually wants -- a beam, or a quiet corner --
  instead of pointing it at a flowerbed.

### Removed
- **The old "Pets for this room" button is gone**, replaced by the Room filter
  above. It tried to read the furniture around you, but the game hands an addon
  the contents of your whole indoors as one lump -- there is no way to ask what
  is in the room you are standing in, so the kitchen, the bedroom and the study
  all voted in one ballot. Choosing the room yourself is both simpler and right.
- **The By Pet / By Spot switch is gone too.** With Room as a filter there is
  only one list, so there is nothing left to switch between.

### Fixed
- **A pet on its decor now sits on it.** Put Akiki on the Loyal Companion's
  Plinth and it sank in up to its chest; put Alarm-o-Dog there and it hovered a
  hand's width above. The stage was handing each pet its seat height in the
  wrong units, so every pet landed at the seat multiplied by its own size --
  small pets low, big pets high, and only the ones near player size where they
  belonged. Every pet now lands on the surface it was given, and the Paw Pal
  Bed seats them on the mattress rather than on the crown of its backrest.
- **And the plinth is the same size on every card.** The point of putting a
  pet on the plinth is to see how big it is, so the camera no longer refits
  itself to each pet: each decor gets one fixed frame, and a mouse and a
  moonkin are seen against the same plinth. Only a pet whose idle outgrows
  that frame -- a rearing eel, a buzzard hovering high -- pushes the camera
  back, rather than being cropped at the top.

## [3.28.0] - 2026-08-25 (August 2026)

### Added
- **Search your pets by name or by kind.** House > Pets has a search box under the
  By Pet / By Spot buttons. Type a pet's name, or type what it IS -- "squirrel",
  "moth", "golem", "murloc" -- and the list narrows to match. The kind is printed
  beside every pet in the list, so whatever you can read there, you can type. As
  you type, a row of suggestions appears offering the kinds that start with those
  letters, with a count of how many you own; click one to fill it in. Two letters
  is usually enough to get from 1,934 pets to the handful you meant.
- **Bats are their own group.** They used to sit with the birds, which never felt
  right -- you hang a bat in a cellar, not on a garden perch. There is now a Bat
  group of its own, and the card tells you a bat wants a rafter somewhere dim.
  The room matcher treats them accordingly: ask "pets for this room" in something
  gothic and the bats put their hands up.
- **The pet card came to the Decor tab.** Decor > Pets now shows the same card the
  Menagerie does -- the live stage with the bed, the plinth and your own character,
  the facts line, and the behaviour flowchart -- instead of the old flat preview.
  One pet, one card, wherever you are looking at it from.

### Changed
- **Pets are grouped correctly now.** A pass over every pet in the game moved
  around two hundred of them into the group they actually belong to. Crocodiles
  were filed as birds. A wombat was a bird. Revenants, which are elementals, were
  filed as insects, as were stone giants, a cauldron, a pen, a torch and a
  carnivorous plant. A panther was an insect. A crate was a rodent. A dog in a
  hard hat was a construct, and a clockwork dog was a bird. Balloon animals were
  filed as the animals they are shaped like. If you have ever picked a group and
  wondered why something odd was in it, that is why.
- **The pet list is in alphabetical order.** It used to be sorted by height, which
  made sense when each row carried a size bar; the size story moved to the card's
  stage, and a list you arrive at knowing the name you want should be in name
  order. Renamed pets sort under the name you gave them.

### Fixed
- **The sound button on a pet's card now works properly.** Clicking the sound chip
  plays a different one of that pet's sounds each time rather than the same, often
  near-silent, loop; a small bar fills to show how long the clip runs; and it no
  longer draws that bar when the client refuses to play anything. The chip also
  stops naming the sound after an internal file name -- it now tells you how many
  other pets share that voice, which is the surprising part.
- **The Pets tab no longer keeps showing the old list after you filter it.** Under
  some combinations of filters the list could stay as it was instead of narrowing.

## [3.27.0] - 2026-08-25 (August 2026)

### Added
- **The Pets tab grew into a Menagerie.** House > Pets is a new view for the whole
  collection, built around one question: where does this pet belong in your home?
  Browse by kind, by family, by size, or by the deeper family tree -- every rat,
  every firefly, every construct -- or flip it around and start from the spot:
  "a shelf", "somewhere that glows", "stays quiet", and see which pets fit. Every
  pet gets a live stage. On its own it fills the frame like a portrait; seat it on
  the Paw Pal Bed or the Loyal Companion's Plinth, or stand your own character
  beside it, and everything renders at true size -- a hand-sized firefly really is
  a speck against your boots, and the facts line tells you exactly how tall it
  stands next to you. Under the stage, a small flowchart shows what the pet does
  when left alone: how it idles, which rare flourishes it breaks into, whether it
  makes noise and how often -- and every node is clickable, playing that animation
  or sound on the stage. A row of chips plays the other tricks it knows: dances,
  sleeps, waves, the chicken dance.

### Fixed
- **Filtering the Placed Decor search no longer trips a red "blocked from an
  action" error.** When your search dimmed the row your mouse happened to be on,
  the addon reached for a Blizzard-only call to switch off that piece's in-world
  glow, and the game blocked it -- once per unlucky hover. The row now simply
  keeps listening until your cursor leaves it, so Blizzard's own code turns the
  glow off, the way it always does. Reported by a player -- thank you.
- **Opening HDG no longer breaks Blizzard's Housing Dashboard.** Two faults, one
  root. Asking the game for your house list before the dashboard had ever opened
  left its panel blank for the whole session -- your house name in the dropdown
  over an empty grey page. And the earlier repair for that blank panel had a worse
  side effect: the dashboard's own Teleport Home button could fail with the red
  "blocked from an action only available to the Blizzard UI" error, the kind that
  offers to disable the addon. The repair is gone. Instead, HDG now wakes the
  dashboard up before asking the game for anything housing-shaped, so it always
  hears its own answers and fills itself the normal way.

## [3.26.0] - 2026-08-24 (August 2026)

### Fixed
- **Vendors who trade in more than one place now show up in each of them.** A vendor
  standing in both housing neighbourhoods -- or in Stormwind and Dornogal, or Darnassus
  and the Ruins of Gilneas -- was only ever listed in one, because the addon kept just the
  last place the game named and quietly dropped the rest. Around 30 vendor listings come
  back, each with its own location. Some of the restored ones have no map coordinates yet,
  so they list without a pin until someone walks them. It showed up as a vendor standing
  in Razorwind Shores that the addon insisted was in Founder's Point.
- **The Pets tab could come up empty, and offer to summon a pet already standing
  next to you.** After a reload it waited for the game to volunteer a pet-journal
  update before building its list, which on a quiet login never came -- so the tab
  said you had no pets, and a companion already out was offered a "Summon" button
  that did nothing when clicked. It now reads your collection at startup like every
  other part of the addon.
- **The Goblin export wrote nonsense for anything sold at a loss.** A negative
  profit came out as "-13.65.44" instead of "-12.34.56" -- so the rows the export
  exists to help you find were the rows you could not read.
- **A pet with no preview showed you nothing at all.** Rather than falling back to
  the icon and saying "Preview unavailable", the panel went completely blank until
  you clicked a different pet. It could also frame a pet with the camera meant for
  furniture.
- **Profession filters went wrong on non-English clients.** A scanned recipe stored
  your profession's translated name over the addon's own, so the Alchemy filter
  found nothing and the Recipes tab counted zero beside a phantom second bucket.
- **Clicking an achievement link in combat blamed the addon.** The red "interface
  action failed" message named Housing Decor Guide; the link now waits, as the map
  buttons beside it already did.
- **Being traded lumber counted as farming it.** A guildmate handing you 40 Ashwood
  started a farming session and dropped a harvest marker where you stood. Buying
  lumber on the auction house did the same.
- **A guild scan left your profession window rearranged.** It borrows your filters
  while it reads the guild's recipes and puts them back when it finishes -- but a
  reload or logout partway through meant it never got to, and those settings persist,
  so the window stayed that way for good. It now restores them the next time you open
  a profession. A scan also no longer stalls ten seconds per profession your guild
  rank cannot view, and the guild's window no longer flashes up full-size on the first
  profession of a session.
- **Capture Floors worked while you were standing in someone else's house**, filing
  their layout under a house of yours. It now asks for a house you own.
- **Moving a piece of decor could be recorded as placing a new one**, and a piece you
  picked up and then cancelled stayed pending until something else was placed.
- **The room-search box in the house editor** left a piece highlighted in the world
  when you filtered it out from under the cursor, and kept working in the background
  after you closed the editor.

### Changed
- **Decor that a vendor sells now says so, even when a quest or achievement also
  gives it.** Hundreds of pieces are both -- and the source line used to name the
  quest, which is true exactly once and no help at all to the person reading your
  blueprint list who never had that quest offered to them. The vendor is standing
  in a zone they can fly to today, so that is what gets named now, with the zone
  beside it. The quest or achievement has not gone anywhere: it still shows on the
  chips, so a piece you can earn as well as buy still says both. This runs
  everywhere one source has to be named -- the Blueprints copy list, the decor
  tooltip, and the item's detail panel -- so the three finally agree with each
  other. The tooltip goes further and lists every vendor that stocks the piece,
  which it could not do while a quest held the line.

## [3.25.0] - 2026-08-21 (August 2026)

### Added
- **The bulk-buy window can be moved, and dismissed for a vendor.** Drag it out of
  the way of the merchant behind it, and close it with the X if you would rather
  buy the ordinary way -- right-clicking goes back to Blizzard's one-at-a-time
  purchase for the rest of that visit. Walk away and come back and the window is
  offered again, so turning it down once is not a decision you have to undo.
- **A blueprint now tells you what it will cost you.** The band that says whether
  a build fits your house now also carries the price of everything you are still
  missing, so you can judge a share code before committing to it rather than
  after. Housing decor is not bought with gold alone, so each currency is counted
  separately -- hover for the breakdown. Decor with no listed price is called out
  rather than quietly left out of the total. Asked for by Madailein Hatter.
- **Copy list, on the Blueprints tab.** Next to the share code you hand
  out, there is now a plain readable list of everything the build is made of --
  rooms, decor, dyes and fixtures, with where each piece of decor comes from --
  ready to paste under your post on Discord, Reddit or a comment thread. Decor a
  pet can be placed on gets its own heading. The list describes the blueprint
  rather than your collection, so it reads the same for whoever picks it up: the
  counts are what the build uses, not what you happen to be short of. Asked for
  by madaileinhatter, who was typing one out by hand to make a blueprint card.

### Changed
- **Every themed room Blizzard has shipped can now be placed in the Architect.**
  The Stormwind, Silvermoon, Bel'ameth and Orgrimmar rooms, both Westfall barns
  and the Orgrimmar Council and Stone Pit rooms were all missing from the room
  palette -- the Architect only knew the plain squares, octagons and hallways.
  They arrive in the room palette with their real sizes, their own artwork and
  Blizzard's own names and ordering, and the two-floor rooms correctly take two
  floors. Thanks to reganart for asking where they were.

- **A blueprint's contents are now listed alphabetically.** They used to arrive in
  whatever order the game sent them, so the dyes on a big build read Purple, Red,
  Blue, Black, White, Brown, Green and a two-hundred-piece decor list was hopeless
  to search by eye. Each group is now in name order, on screen and in the copied
  list alike. Thanks to madaileinhatter for pointing it out.

### Fixed
- **Buying in bulk now works at coupon vendors.** Right-clicking a decor item at
  a vendor opens the quantity picker, but it only ever did that for things priced
  in gold -- anything bought with Community Coupons was left out, which is most of
  what the neighbourhood vendors sell. The picker now counts your coupons the same
  way it counts your gold, tells you when a stack costs more than you have, and
  counts each purchase as it actually arrives -- coupon decor lands in your bags
  rather than straight into decor storage, so it had nothing to count before.
  Thanks to madaileinhatter for asking.
- **Materials reach the Auction House search bar again.** Shift-click a material
  -- in the Warehouse list or in a recipe's materials -- and it lands in the
  auction house search box, which is what clicking one used to do. It moved to
  shift because a plain click now picks the material and shows what it is used
  in, and the two were quietly fighting over the same click. The hover tells you
  about it, since nothing on the row does. Thanks to JodouKast for reporting it.

- **The Lumber Tracker no longer stops tracking after a trip to the bank.** Visit
  a mailbox, vendor, bank or trade window and, if the game did not tell the addon
  the window had closed again, the tracker quietly gave up for the rest of the
  session: it stopped following the lumber you were farming, and its numbers sat
  still until you opened the main window. A reload was the only way back. It now
  checks whether one of those windows is actually open instead of waiting to be
  told it closed, so there is nothing left to get stuck. Thanks to pxspin for
  reporting it.

## [3.24.1] - 2026-08-20 (August 2026)

### Fixed
- Blizzard's Housing Dashboard no longer opens blank after using HDG. Asking the
  game for your house list, which the House tab does, left the dashboard's own
  panel waiting on a message it had already missed, so it opened empty while still
  showing your house name in the dropdown. HDG now repairs that when the dashboard
  opens.

### Fixed
- Buying a single item from the vendor quantity picker now finishes cleanly. The
  picker counted an item as bought the moment the purchase was sent rather than
  when it arrived in decor storage, so a purchase of one showed nothing left to
  buy while the button still read Stop, and a purchase that was stopped or that
  never arrived was reported as if it had succeeded. Larger purchases counted one
  ahead the whole way for the same reason.

## [3.24.0] - 2026-08-19 (August 2026)

### Added
- **Nael Silvertongue now has a waypoint.** The Silvermoon City vendor who sells
  the Ornate Sin'dorei Sconce was in the guide but had no location, so there was
  nothing to navigate to. Thanks to ReganB for spotting it and sending the
  coordinates.

- **Material hovers now show which of your characters is holding the mats.**
  Hover a material on the Warehouse or Recipes list and you get one list of
  every character with some, highest first, with the shared Warband Bank on its
  own line at the bottom. The game will not let an addon read another
  character's bags, so each character's figures are recorded while you are
  playing it and the hover says so -- alt counts are from that character's last
  login. Bank figures only refresh on characters where you have actually opened
  a bank, so a character you have never banked on keeps its last known number
  instead of quietly reading as empty. Thanks to TMcGee92102 for asking.

- **The Lumber Stock table's hover now lists your other characters too.** The
  table's own columns already show what the character you are playing is
  carrying and what is in the Warband Bank, so the hover adds the part it
  cannot: which of your other characters are sitting on that lumber, and how
  much. It appears under the harvesting milestone, and only when somebody else
  actually has some.

- **Full item tooltips on material rows, if you want them** (Settings >
  Helpers > Tooltips). Material rows deliberately show HDG's own compact
  breakdown rather than the game's item tooltip, which is why other addons have
  had nothing to attach to there. Turn this on and the game's item tooltip
  renders above HDG's breakdown, which also gives addons like Altoholic and the
  auction addons somewhere to add their own lines. Off by default -- it makes
  every material hover considerably taller.

## [3.23.0] - 2026-08-18 (August 2026)

### Added
- **A Pets filter in the Decor browser, for the pets you can place in a house.**
  12.1 lets some pets sit on certain decor -- pet beds, plinths, a dog house --
  and a Pets button at the end of the filter row lists the ones in your own
  collection that can. The list runs smallest to largest, because size is the
  thing no in-game window will tell you: each row carries the pet's height and a
  bar measuring it against your own character, so you can pick something that
  suits a windowsill rather than a courtyard. The Tags row becomes the ten pet
  families, the search box searches your pets by name, and the panel on the
  right shows the pet at full size with a Summon or Dismiss button beneath it.

  Heights are measured from each pet's model, because the game does not report
  one. Eleven pets have models that cannot be measured; those show no height and
  sort to the end of the list rather than showing a number that would be wrong.

- **The Mogul table exports to CSV again.** An Export button sits with the
  filters; it opens a window with the table as comma-separated text, ready to
  paste into a spreadsheet or send to somebody. What you get is what you are
  looking at -- the same filters, the same sort order -- so narrowing to one
  profession and exporting gives you that list and nothing else. The columns
  are the ones the old export had, in the same order, so a spreadsheet built
  against it still lines up; the five columns the table has gained since
  (TSM minimum, TSM market, sale rate, sold per day, and quantity on the
  auction house) follow on the end. Thanks to armaniko for asking for it back.

## [3.22.0] - 2026-08-15 (August 2026)

### Added
- **Two filters for decor you can just go and buy.** Under Acquire, a second
  row of buttons sits below Achieve/Rep/Endeavor/Quest, on both Shop by Vendor
  and Find Decor.

  **Neighborhood** shows decor sold by the merchants standing in Founder's
  Point and Razorwind Shores -- the Endeavor vendors among them, so coupon
  decor is in there too. Nothing there is locked behind reputation, a quest or
  an achievement, because you are already where the merchant is.

  **Gold, no unlocks** is wider: decor any vendor sells for gold that needs no
  reputation, quest or achievement first. It reads the requirement off the
  catalog, so a vendor you have to unlock before you can reach them can still
  appear -- Neighborhood is the one to trust when you want certainty.
  (Asked for by ReganB.)

### Changed
- **The Gold Only filter is gone, replaced by the two above.** It answered a
  question about price rather than about access, so it listed promotional and
  in-game shop decor that happens to cost gold, and things sold behind a
  questline. That was rarely what anyone wanted from it.

### Fixed
- **Three Brawl'gar Arena decor items no longer look freely buyable.**
  Brawler's Barricade, the Brawler's Guild Punching Bag and the Champion
  Brawler's Gloves all require an arena rank, which the game's own decor
  catalog does not mention anywhere -- so they read as ordinary gold purchases.

- **Aegis can send a blueprint's missing pieces straight to a shopping list.**
  If you run Aegis, its guild blueprint library now has a button that hands
  over what a build needs and what you are short of; Housing Decor Guide works
  out where each piece comes from and builds a list named after the build.
  Sending the same build again refreshes that list rather than making a second
  one. Nothing changes if you do not run Aegis. (12.1)

## [3.21.1] - 2026-08-14 (August 2026)

### Added
- **Four more 12.1 decor vendors can be mapped to.** Firetender Zab'ni and
  Jan'sari the Watchful on The Coiled Isle, the Skull of Er'inye down in the
  Vaults of Atal'Utek, and Unquestionably Griftah in the neighbourhood now carry
  positions, so they get a pin and a waypoint instead of only a name. Reported
  by soteirian. The rest of the 12.1 sellers are still listed without a pin
  until someone walks them.
- **Twenty decor recipes added in patch 12.1 are listed for the first time.**
  Blacksmithing, Leatherworking, Enchanting, Engineering, Jewelcrafting,
  Inscription, Tailoring and Alchemy each gained decor the addon had never heard
  of, so they were missing from the Recipes tab entirely.

### Changed
- **The recipe costs that ship with the addon are now patch 12.1's.** Most decor
  recipes were re-costed in 12.1 -- Thalassian Lumber roughly halved across the
  board, and several recipes gained or lost a material outright -- and until now
  only players who had run a profession scan saw the new figures. Fifty-eight
  recipes are corrected, so the Recipes tab, the queue and the shopping list all
  start from the right numbers whether you have scanned or not.

### Fixed
- **Recipe tooltips now quote the same material amounts as the Materials panel.**
  A profession scan picks up 12.1's costs, but the tooltip was still reading the
  amounts that shipped with the addon -- so hovering a recipe and reading the
  panel beside it gave two different answers.
- **The Mogul price refresh was reading that same stale list**, so a reagent a
  recipe only needs since 12.1 could go unpriced.

## [3.21.0] - 2026-08-12 (August 2026)

### Added
- **Capturing your house now lays the map out like the real thing.** Rooms land
  on the Architect canvas arranged the way they actually connect -- doors lined
  up, neighbours adjacent, stairwell shafts kept clear on the floors above --
  instead of a flat row you rebuilt by hand. Where the game genuinely can't
  say which of two arrangements you built (it happens with a couple of room
  combinations per house), the closest valid one is picked and a quick drag
  fixes it; everything stays connected and nothing overlaps.
- Rooms of the same type now show their number ("Hallway 1", "Hallway 2") on
  the canvas again, so twins are tellable apart.
- **Upper floors now capture properly too.** Stairwells arrive as what they
  really are -- one room per floor, stacked -- so walking your floors in Layout
  mode no longer tangles them into phantom duplicates that refused to move.
  Rooms on upper floors land on top of the rooms that hold them up, stairwell
  towers line up vertically with the floor below, and nothing gets laid out
  inside a garden's open sky.
- **The 12.1 housing vendors are in.** Thirty vendors the game added in 12.1 --
  Timicky, Agratha, Griftah, Perry Winkles, Cursed Keepsake and the rest,
  across Founder's Point and Razorwind Shores. Their decor was already named
  in tooltips but they were missing from the Zone Scanner and Shop by Vendor
  entirely, so a whole neighbourhood's worth of items had no visible seller.
- Their map coordinates are not in yet, so they show up in the lists but have
  no map pin. "Map All" and "Open vendor on map" quietly skip them rather than
  dropping a marker in the wrong place; the pins follow once each one is walked.
- If the addon's files are only half there, it now says so in one line at login
  instead of filling your screen with errors. An incomplete install used to
  surface as a couple of dozen identical-looking Lua errors and a missing
  minimap button, with nothing pointing at the cause. It now names the folders
  that came up empty and tells you to reinstall -- and if your WoW sits inside
  Dropbox or OneDrive, to move it out first, because sync clients can report a
  folder as fully synced while leaving it empty.

### Fixed
- The buy-quantity dial at a decor vendor now opens on 1 instead of 000, so
  Buy is live the moment it appears. It used to say "Buy 0" and do nothing
  until you clicked a wheel.
- "Set quantity" on a shopping list item now opens with 1 already in the box,
  selected -- so Enter is enough for the common case. It used to open empty.
- Clicking an achievement in an item's source line opens the achievement again
  instead of throwing an error. Patch 12.1 removed the call the link used.
- Opening Layout mode while standing on an upper storey saved those rooms one
  floor too low -- and on the ground floor, quietly saved nothing at all. Both
  now land on the right floor.
- "Expand Stairwell up" plans no longer vanish the next time the house is
  captured, the option now also appears on the Stairwell Room (Empty) -- it
  exists in the game, so it exists here -- and it no longer shows on stairwells
  already at the roof.
- The floor check in the Architect panel now catches rooms overlapping a
  garden or stairwell rising from a lower floor -- previously it said "Valid"
  for exactly the overlaps dragging would refuse.
- Duplicate rooms on different floors now tell apart in the detail panel
  ("Hallway 1-2" vs "Hallway 2-1" -- floor, then number).
- Capturing all floors now stops with a clear message if the game won't say
  how many floors the house has, instead of quietly forgetting every floor
  above the first.
- Stairwells placed in what-if designs before this update keep their two
  storeys (a data-model change would have silently shrunk them to one).
- Expanding a stairwell up and then capturing that floor for real no longer
  fight each other -- the plan hands over to the captured room cleanly.
- Torv Dubstomp's waypoint pointed at empty ground. Both his Map and Waypoint
  buttons were working from a position recorded a zone away from where he
  actually stands, so following them dropped you near Frosthoof Watch instead
  of Thunder Totem. He and Ransa Greyfeather now both carry positions taken
  standing next to them.

## [3.20.0] - 2026-08-01 (August 2026)

### Added
- **A new colour theme: Pumpkin Spice.** Warm ivory and rose gold on deep
  forest and charcoal, edged in pumpkin -- an autumn palette to sit alongside
  the cool ones. It is built the other way round from every other theme here:
  the forest is the frame and the cards sit *inset* into it rather than raised
  off it, which keeps every panel near-black so decor names, swatches and
  prices carry. The five core colours are Madailein Hatter's, shared and named
  by them and used with their permission; the pumpkin, amber, oxblood and moss
  that fill out the autumn range were added on top of those. Pick it in
  Settings.
- The Placed Decor list in Advanced mode now has a search box. Type part of a
  name and everything that does not match fades back, so the piece you are after
  stands out in a long list. Matching rows still work exactly as they did --
  hover to highlight the decor, click to select it, then move or remove it with
  the usual controls.
- The list is not shortened, only faded. Blizzard restricts changing what that
  panel contains, and removing rows outright makes the remaining ones
  unselectable, which would cost you the very thing you opened it for.
- The fading approach is Liberty's, author of the DoorSearch addon, used with
  their permission. If you already run DoorSearch you do not need both.

### Fixed
- The sidebar now keeps the section you are in on screen. Tabs set the window
  height, and the shorter ones -- Move Planner most of all -- left the sidebar
  too short to show its lower half, so clicking Move Planner scrolled the
  entry you just picked out of sight. Whichever entry is lit is now scrolled
  back into view whenever the sidebar changes, so you can always see where you
  are and what sits either side of it.

## [3.19.1] - 2026-07-26 (July 2026)

### Fixed
- Placed Decor list: corrected the description of what it covers. The list is
  scoped to the area you are in -- indoors or outdoors -- not to a single room, so
  every interior room shares one list. The wording in v3.19.0 said "room", which
  would have had you expecting the list to change as you walked between rooms.

## [3.19.0] - 2026-07-26 (July 2026)

### Added
- House Editor companion: the Snapshots tab is now **Placed**, and it opens with a
  live **Placed Decor** list showing everything you have put down in the area you
  are in -- indoors or outdoors -- with a count per item. Search it by name to
  settle the question the editor cannot answer: did I actually place that, and how
  many? Step outside and the list switches to your exterior decor. Your saved snapshots sit underneath it,
  unchanged. The list is built fresh each session, so it never shows decor from a
  house you have left.

  One thing it deliberately does not do: clicking an entry will not select or
  highlight that piece out in the world. Blizzard restricts selecting and removing
  placed decor to their own interface, so that part has to be done from the Placed
  Decor list in Advanced mode -- ours is there to tell you what is down and how
  much of it, which is the part their list cannot answer. Blizzard have said the
  restriction may be lifted later; if it is, this list will do the rest.
  (Thanks ReganB and Madailein Hatter!)

### Fixed
- Essence of Lumber tracker: characters you've hidden on the Alts tab now count toward the account-wide total and appear in the hover breakdown. Hiding an alt is meant to keep it out of the professions grid, not to remove it from your essence tally -- the tracker was under-counting for anyone who hides their unlevelled alts. (Thanks Pixie!)
- Vendors: Woodsman Drake (Grizzly Hills) is now correctly listed as Alliance instead of Neutral, so the guide no longer points cross-faction players toward an Alliance hub. (Thanks Haisul!)
- Blueprints (12.1): the interior pet-decor budget meter now shows its full number. The pet caps rose to 100 interior / 25 exterior, and the interior column was too narrow, so "100" was clipped to "1...". (Thanks Madailein Hatter!)
- Blueprints (12.1): the house's floor count is read correctly again after the game removed the old floor-count API, so a multi-floor house captures every floor into Projects instead of only the ground floor.

## [3.18.1] - 2026-07-22 (July 2026)

### Added
- Acquire: shift-click an item in the Find Decor list to link it in chat, the same as the Decor Browser.

## [3.18.0] - 2026-07-21 (July 2026)

### Added
- Decor Browser: shift-click a decor row to link it in chat, the same as shift-clicking any item.

### Changed
- Decor Browser: sending a decor's recipe to the craft queue is now ctrl-click (it was shift-click, which now links the item in chat). The row tooltip and the on-screen hints show the new bindings.

## [3.17.2] - 2026-07-20 (July 2026)

### Fixed
- Non-English game clients: professions now display correctly on the Alts tab's Account Summary, and the Battle for Azeroth column now appears across the profession views. HDG had been matching professions and expansions by their English names, so on a localized client (e.g. Spanish) the summary grid came up empty and BfA didn't register. It now matches on stable IDs and localized names, and existing characters fill in automatically on your next login -- no rescan needed. (Thanks to the reporter on a Spanish client!)
- Corrected Toraan the Revered's vendor pin to the Vindicaar map you arrive on when you zone in.
- Minimap button: HDG no longer re-anchors its minimap button every time the window opens. This stops the button "jumping" for players who use a minimap-button manager like SexyMap (the button now stays wherever SexyMap places it). (Thanks Paulo!)

## [3.17.1] - 2026-07-18 (July 2026)

### Fixed
- Vendor map pins got a full audit (Verified standing at each vendor in-game) and 12 pins were corrected. Highlights: Provisioner Aristta moved to her real home in Thaldraszus (she was pinned in the Azure Span), Captain Donald Adams now points at the present-day captain in Orgrimmar (no Zidormi time-phasing required), Val'zuun and Halenthos Brightstride now pin the correct Dalaran maps, "Yen" Malone and "High Tides" Ren no longer share another vendor cluster's spot on Razorwind Shores, and Thraxadar pins his actual perch on Slayer's Rise. Also nudged: Mothkeeper Wew'tam, Morta Gage, Brave Tuho, Paul North, and the Brawlpub pair in the Deeprun Tram.
- Removed "Bad Luck" Symmes from the vendor list -- he's been replaced by Paul North in the Brawl'gar Arena, who sells the same decor.
- Fixed Falara Nightsong (Fel Hammer decor vendor) being pinned to the wrong map entirely -- her map pin and waypoint now point at the actual demon hunter hall.
- Fixed Second Chair Pawdo's vendor pin in Dornogal sitting about 135 yards from where he actually stands.
- Future-proofed the decor catalog against Blizzard removing the housing-catalog compatibility shim: HDG now reads the current API fields directly (owned counts, destroy identities). No visible change today; prevents a breakage when the deprecated bridge is deleted (and fixes HDG for anyone running with deprecation fallbacks disabled).
- Fixed the decor catalog failing to load when the game hands back a corrupted catalog row (seen once in the wild) -- HDG now skips the unreadable row with a note in the Debug log and loads the other ~1,800 items normally.

## [3.17.0] - 2026-07-17 (July 2026)

> Note: the recipe-data changes in this release should not change anything you see on 12.0.7 -- the live capture matches the built-in database. This build is PTR-compatible: if you'd like to test the new recipe harvesting against 12.1.0's changes, install it on the PTR and open a profession (or hit Scan Recipes).

### Added
- Zone alerts: the zone-entry chat message got a glow-up -- vendor-pin icon, gold branding, a clear headline -- and now ends with a clickable [View] link that opens the Zone Scanner with that list. It also fires reliably on fresh logins without opening HDG first. (Thanks Woodymakespizza!)
- Recipes: a new Scan Recipes button on the Recipes title harvests decor recipe data from every profession in your guild -- guildmates don't need to be online. One click keeps recipe costs current across all professions without switching alts. The button shows live progress and a second click cancels; it only updates recipe data, never who-knows-what.
- Recipes: recipe materials are now read live from your profession book -- including the sub-recipes behind them, so the Raw materials view, Craft These First list and readiness percentages all track the live game too. Material quality tiers are captured as groups, so any tier you hold counts toward a recipe (recipes list the cheapest tier). Opening a profession captures every decor recipe's reagents, and the Recipes, Materials, Lumber and Mogul views use the captured data over the built-in database -- so when a patch changes crafting costs (or adds brand-new decor recipes), HDG reflects it as soon as you open the profession, no addon update needed.
- Decor: the number you own now shows on every decor row, all the time -- not just in the Destroy view -- so you can see how many copies you have while browsing. It sits in a tidy right-hand column, and the craftable star tucks into its top-left corner.
- Decor: the detail panel has two new buttons beside Wishlist -- Map and Waypoint -- that route you straight to a decor's vendor (same actions as Shop by Vendor). They appear only for decor a vendor actually sells.

### Changed
- The per-zone waypoint button in the Shopping list and the Zone tab now uses the housing vendor icon.

### Fixed
- Decor: destroying decor you own in dyed colors now works reliably in the Stored view. An item you own only in dyed colors no longer shows a phantom "undyed" row with a count that won't destroy -- the undyed row appears only when you genuinely have undyed copies, and its number now matches what you actually own.
- Decor: items you own a single copy of now appear in the Stored view (they were being hidden), and you can destroy your last stored copy instead of one always being left behind.
- Decor: the search box now clears when you press Reset (the text used to stay put even though the list had already been unfiltered).
- Recipes: corrected the built-in reagent data for 8 recipes that listed the wrong quality tier of a material (Serevite Ore, Draconium Ore, Sundered Onyx, Eternity Amber) -- material counts for those recipes now match your bags. Verified against a live in-game capture of all 305 recipes.

## [3.16.0] - 2026-07-15 (July 2026)

### Added
- Blueprints: the budget meters now include Interior and Exterior pet-decor budgets (12.1), shown as two slimmer bars beside the room and decor meters, with the game's pet icon and the full budget name on hover.
- Blueprints: a blueprint icon now sits to the left of the "Blueprints" heading above the list.

### Fixed
- HDG could fail to load on the 12.1 client: the game renamed a housing event, and registering the old name aborted startup (which then left the main window unable to build). HDG now skips any housing event that isn't valid on your game version, so a rename like this can't stop it loading.
- Blueprints: clicking a blueprint no longer errors on 12.1. The game changed how a blueprint reports its budget costs, and HDG now reads the new format.
- Blueprints: a budget the blueprint doesn't use now reads "0" instead of "-1" (for example, the exterior-decor meter on a room blueprint).
- Blueprints: if the game fails to send your blueprint list, HDG now shows the reason instead of leaving the browser silently empty.

## [3.15.0] - 2026-07-14 (July 2026)

### Added
- A "Reset minimap button position" button in Settings (under "Reset all settings to defaults"). Use it if the HDG minimap button goes missing or keeps snapping to the lower-left corner -- it puts the button back at a visible spot so you can drag it where you want. (This can happen after a game crash clears the button's saved position.)

### Changed
- The "Mark collected decor at vendors" setting tooltip now shows the actual check and plus markers (and notes they sit in the item's top-right corner), so it matches what you see on the vendor.

### Fixed
- Destroying a dyed decor variant now works. The Destroy button previously did nothing for a dyed copy because it targeted the undyed base stack; it now destroys the specific dyed variant you selected.
- The decor list now updates immediately after destroying a dyed variant, instead of needing a reload to reflect the new count.
- Corrected T'lama's map location: Show on Map placed the pin outside the Great Seal in Dazar'alor instead of on the vendor inside it (thanks Gnuclear Gnome).

## [3.14.1] - 2026-07-13 (July 2026)

### Changed
- Blueprints: a UX review pass over the whole tab. The action buttons grey out until a blueprint is selected, and an empty tab now says what to do ("Paste a share code, or pick a blueprint from your catalog") instead of showing a blank panel. The fit verdict is a proper badge (green when a build fits, red with the game's reason when it doesn't). The house picker now always names the house the numbers were computed against, even before you touch it. Content group headers turn amber when they contain missing items, so scrolling the group bars alone shows where the gaps are. The guidance line moved above the action buttons and a small "Rename:" caption marks the rename box.
- Blueprints: the missing count shown in the verdict and item tally now counts only items that can actually be acquired (decor and dyes) -- locked rooms, fixtures, and house types no longer inflate the number that Route to Shopping promises.
- Blueprints: Route to Shopping now opens the shopping widget on the routed list, so the result is visible instead of just a status message. Saving a blueprint shows the name you typed immediately instead of flashing the share code while the catalog refreshes.
- Blueprints: renaming an Automatic Save backup now says backups can't be renamed instead of silently reverting; the remove "x" on every row explains itself on hover (delete from catalog vs forget a pasted code); and the Apply to House tooltip notes that each apply stores an Automatic Save backup that uses a blueprint slot.
- Blueprints: Route to Shopping is styled as the tab's primary action, and clicking a blueprint that's still loading no longer restarts its request timer (which quietly postponed the "still waiting" notice and the timeout).
- Blueprints: the budget meters use the game's own icons (rooms / interior decor / exterior decor, same as the Import dialog) with the full budget name on hover; owned items show a checkmark instead of the word "have"; and the Rename box no longer ghosts its placeholder text under a blueprint's actual name.
- Buttons disabled by the addon (like the Blueprints action row with nothing selected, or Buy All with an empty list) now visibly fade instead of just ignoring clicks.
- The Blueprints tab's guidance line now reminds you to Save Blueprint after applying if you want the build in your catalog (applying alone doesn't save it).

### Fixed
- Automatic Save backups no longer count toward your blueprint slot total, so the count matches the game's own dashboard (it was over-reporting by the number of backups you had).
- Corrected the map and coordinates for several decor vendors whose records pointed at older versions of the NPC, so Show on Map could land on the wrong spot: First Arcanist Thalyssra, Moon Priestess Lasara, Lifecaller Tzadrak, Ta'sam, and Celestine of the Harvest.

## [3.14.0] - 2026-07-12 (July 2026)

### Added
- Blueprints tab (House > Blueprints) for the 12.1 Midnight housing update. The tab appears automatically on a 12.1 client -- nothing changes on current retail. Paste any blueprint share code to see exactly what's inside -- rooms, decor, dyes, fixtures -- and what you're missing, checked against whichever of your houses you pick. Pasted codes are kept in your own library across sessions and can be given private names; your saved Blizzard blueprint catalog shows alongside with rename and delete built in.
- Blueprints: Route to Shopping turns a blueprint's missing items into a shopping list named after the blueprint (re-routing refreshes the same list), ready for the vendor tools and Buy All. Import as Set files the contents into Styles as a furnishing set, and House/Interior blueprints can open in the Architect.
- Blueprints: budget meters show whether a build fits the target house's room, interior, and exterior budgets -- Room blueprints are measured against the house's remaining headroom, since they add to what's already placed. When an import would be blocked, the tab shows Blizzard's own reason (wrong faction for the neighborhood, locked house type or size, missing rooms or dyes...).
- Blueprints: Save Blueprint snapshots your current house from inside the tab (Full House, This Room, Interior, or Exterior), Apply to House opens Blizzard's import dialog prefilled with the selected code, and Link in chat drops a clickable blueprint link others can import from. Note that applying a blueprint doesn't save it to your catalog -- use Save Blueprint afterwards to keep a copy.
- Blueprints: full-house and exterior blueprints color by faction in the list (Alliance blue, Horde red) once inspected, so mixed collections stay readable at a glance.
- Vendor names in the shopping widget and the zone scanner are now links: hover underlines the name, and clicking it jumps straight to that vendor in Shop by Vendor (opening the main window if needed). Clicking anywhere else on the row still collapses the group (thanks Vector).

### Changed
- The profession window buttons are now compact icons instead of text buttons: the HDG logo opens the Decor Guide, and a decor-vendor icon toggles the house-decor recipe filter (it glows while the filter is active).

### Fixed
- Vendors new in 12.0.7 that HDG doesn't have exact coordinates for yet (Zuronar and friends) now show their zone map in the vendor panel instead of an empty box. The map draws without a location pin -- the catalog only tells us the zone, not where in it the vendor stands -- and pins will appear as coordinates get added.
- The "Decor Guide" and "Filter Decor" buttons on the profession window are visible again. They were rendering underneath the profession window's own frame layer, so only their hover tooltip showed (thanks draculja_aka_hellsreaper).
- Caydori Brightstar (Monk order hall vendor) now shows the correct map, The Wandering Isle, instead of The Eternal Palace. Her vendor record had the wrong map ID.

## [3.13.0] - 2026-07-08 (July 2026)

### Added
- Essence of Lumber tracker: an icon in the title bar shows how many Essence of Lumber you hold across your whole warband, with a mouseover listing which characters have them (and how many, and whether any sit in the bank). Because Essence is soulbound and can't be moved between characters, the total tells you at a glance where your stock actually is. The icon greys out when you have none (thanks Pixie).
- Shopping list "Buy All" at vendors: with the shopping widget open at any vendor that stocks your list items, one click buys everything at the full list quantity. A single summary confirmation replaces the game's per-item non-refundable popup, and purchases are paced so they land directly in decor storage. As each item lands it is checked off the active shopping list, so the list draws down to what's still needed. Only gold-priced decor is bulk-bought for now -- currency-priced list items (community coupons etc.) are skipped with a note and can still be bought individually at the vendor.
- Right-click a gold-priced decor item at any vendor for a quantity picker: dial in how many with three digit wheels, see the running total against your gold and decor-storage space, and buy them all in one go. Items sold for a currency (community coupons etc.) right-click as normal -- the picker is gold-only for now. Off by default -- enable it under Settings > Helpers > Vendors.

### Changed
- Shopping list: re-importing a collection you already have now updates that list in place instead of stacking a second copy with the same name. Lists that genuinely share a name are numbered ("Void Collection (2)") so the switcher dropdown is always unambiguous.
- Shopping list: the "+ New" button moved up to the header next to the list switcher (it was overflowing the bottom button row), and the header summary now reads "N items - N vendors".
- Move Planner: selecting a plot now outlines it in white instead of source-green or target-gold. The old colors matched two of the community plot-letter hues, so a selected yellow-letter plot could read as a green-letter plot (thanks ReganB). Source/target colors still show on the plot list dots, map labels, and tooltips.

### Fixed
